## 2 Related Work

Interactive user-agent learning. ## 3.3 Two-Level Additive Advantage

Th<sub>e</sub> <sub>ou</sub>t<sub>come</sub> b<sub>ranc</sub>h <sub>reuses</sub> $A _ { k } ^ { \mathrm { o } }$ fr<sub>o</sub>m E<sub>qua</sub>ti<sub>o</sub>n 2<sub>.</sub> F<sub>o</sub>r <sub>p</sub>r<sub>o</sub>m<sub>p</sub>t <sub>� an</sub>d U2U i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>ex</sub> $q ,$ l<sub>e</sub>t $\boldsymbol { \mathcal { T } } ( \boldsymbol { x } , \boldsymbol { q } )$ <sub>con</sub>t<sub>a</sub>i<sub>n ro</sub>ll<sub>ou</sub>t<sub>s</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>reac</sub>h <sub>�</sub> <sub>an</sub>d h<sub>ave a va</sub>lid <sub>nex</sub>t<sub>-user reac</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> W<sub>e cons</sub>t<sub>ruc</sub>t th<sub>e a</sub>li<sub>gne</sub>d <sup>reaction</sup> g<sup>ro</sup>up<sup>:</sup>

$$
\mathcal { B } ^ { p } ( x , q ) = \{ f _ { i , q } \ | \ i \in I ( x , q ) \} ,\tag{4}
$$

<sub>an</sub>d <sub>norma</sub>li<sub>ze</sub> <sub>w</sub>ithi<sub>n</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t l<sub>oca</sub>l <sub>compar</sub>i<sub>son</sub> <sub>se</sub>t<sub>:</sub>

$$
A _ { k , q } ^ { \mathrm { p } } = \frac { f _ { k , q } - \mu _ { f } ( x , q ) } { \sigma _ { f } ( x , q ) + \epsilon } .\tag{5}
$$

Th<sub>e</sub> U2U <sub>es</sub>ti<sub>ma</sub>t<sub>or</sub> <sub>uses</sub> <sub>on</sub>l<sub>y</sub> th<sub>e</sub> i<sub>mme</sub>di<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub> <sub>nex</sub>t <sub>reac</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> <sub>an</sub>d d<sub>oes no</sub>t <sub>propaga</sub>t<sub>e</sub> l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>er reac</sub>ti<sub>ons</sub> b<sub>ac</sub>k<sub>war</sub>d<sub>.</sub> Si<sub>ng</sub>l<sub>e</sub>t<sub>on</sub> <sub>or</sub> <sub>cons</sub>t<sub>an</sub>t <sub>anc</sub>h<sub>ors</sub> <sub>rece</sub>i<sub>ve</sub> <sub>zero.</sub> E<sub>very</sub> t<sub>ra</sub>i<sub>na</sub>bl<sub>e</sub> t<sub>o</sub>k<sub>en</sub> i<sub>n</sub> $\boldsymbol { a } _ { k , q }$ <sub>rece</sub>i<sub>ves:</sub>

$$
A _ { k , q } = A _ { k } ^ { \mathrm { o } } + \lambda A _ { k , q } ^ { \mathrm { p } } .\tag{6}
$$

The two terms are com<sub>p</sub>lementar<sub>y</sub>: $A _ { k } ^ { \mathrm { o } }$ <sub>p</sub>reserves t<sup>h</sup>e terminal objective, whereas $A _ { k , q } ^ { \mathrm { p } }$ di<sub>s</sub>ti<sub>ngu</sub>i<sub>s</sub>h<sub>es</sub> l<sub>oca</sub>l <sub>progress</sub> f<sub>rom</sub> f<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> Di<sub>sa</sub>bli<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>e reac</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> b<sub>ranc</sub>h <sub>re</sub>d<sub>uces</sub> E<sub>qua-</sub> ti<sub>on</sub> 6 t<sub>o</sub> th<sub>e s</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c</sub>t <sub>ou</sub>t<sub>come-on</sub>l<sub>y</sub> I<sub>n</sub>t<sub>erac</sub>ti<sub>ve</sub> GRPO <sub>con</sub>t<sub>ro</sub>l<sub>.</sub> I<sub>n</sub> <sub>an</sub> <sub>ou</sub>t<sub>come-</sub>h<sub>omogeneous</sub> <sub>group,</sub> <sub>reac</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> l<sub>a</sub>b<sub>e</sub>l<sub>s</sub> <sub>can</sub> <sub>s</sub>till dif<sub>er</sub> <sub>across</sub> <sub>ro</sub>ll<sub>ou</sub>t<sub>s;</sub> thi<sub>s</sub> i<sub>s</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>mec</sub>h<sub>an</sub>i<sub>ca</sub>l <sub>proper</sub>t<sub>y</sub> <sub>ra</sub>th<sub>er</sub> th<sub>an an</sub> i<sub>n</sub>d<sub>epen</sub>d<sub>en</sub>t <sub>per</sub>f<sub>ormance c</sub>l<sub>a</sub>i<sub>m.</sub>

## 3.4 Optimization and Anchoring

F<sub>or</sub> <sub>ass</sub>i<sub>s</sub>t<sub>an</sub>t t<sub>o</sub>k<sub>ens,</sub> <sub>we</sub> fi<sub>rs</sub>t <sub>c</sub>li<sub>p</sub> th<sub>e</sub> GRPO i<sub>mpor</sub>t<sub>ance</sub> <sub>ra</sub>ti<sub>o</sub> [Schulman et al., 2017]:

$$
\widetilde { \rho } _ { k , t } = \mathrm { c l i p } ( \rho _ { k , t } , 1 - \varepsilon , 1 + \varepsilon ) .\tag{7}
$$

S<sub>u</sub>b<sub>s</sub>tit<sub>u</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> th<sub>e</sub> U2U<sub>-</sub>l<sub>eve</sub>l <sub>a</sub>d<sub>van</sub>t<sub>age</sub> $A _ { k , q }$ th<sub>en</sub> <sub>g</sub>i<sub>ves</sub> th<sub>e</sub> actor objective:

$$
\mathcal { L } ( \boldsymbol { \theta } ) = - \mathbb { E } _ { k , t } \left[ \operatorname* { m i n } \bigl ( \rho _ { k , t } A _ { k , q ( t ) } , \widetilde { \rho } _ { k , t } A _ { k , q ( t ) } \bigr ) \right] .\tag{8}
$$

## 4.2 Experimental Settings

## 5.1 Main Results on the �-Bench Family
